- **kulturni-sezona**: Season planner for Kulturní Přehled. Sends no email. Local-only — needs op, ensembles/*.sh scrapers and a full-power PAT; the cloud routine never runs this. Trigger phrases - "naplánuj sezónu", "season plan", "sezónní scrape". ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-rna-structure))
- **kumichem-sequence-search**: Search supplied oligonucleotide sequences against KumiChem human mature RNA and precursor RNA references. Use for individual or batch complementarity counts and bounded alignment details, with explicit intended-target exclusions and edit distance. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-sequence-search))
- **kumichem-sirna-candidates**: Generate siDirect rule-based siRNA candidate evidence for supplied targets; not learned activity or a complete study. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-sirna-candidates))
- **kumichem-sirna-research**: Design and evaluate complete siRNA research with interactive planning or frozen-method replay; host-owned memory and report review. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-sirna-research))
- **kumichem-skills-v3**: Use KumiChem V3 scientific APIs from an external agent: compose atomic tools, follow a host-executed Interactive workflow, or delegate a complete research run to the hosted Agent Loop. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-skills-v3))
- **kumichem-structrfm**: Extract RNA features or predict secondary structure with released structRFM weights; not potency or toxicity. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-structrfm))
- **kumichem-target-safety**: Retrieve and interpret GWAS positional associations and gnomAD gene constraint for a target; supporting evidence, not a drug-safety score. ([DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills](https://github.com/DLeader-Inc/kumichem-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kumichem-target-safety))

- **kundoku-bungo**: 現代の日本語と、明治〜昭和 20 年ごろの公の書き言葉（漢文訓読体の普通文。法令・勅令・内閣告諭・告示・演説・答辞・新聞の論説などの文体）を相互に変換する。漢字片仮名交じり、濁点を付けない書き方、小書きの仮名を使わない書き方、歴史的仮名遣いにも変換する。求められれば、明治の前期・中期の合字「ヿ」（コト）と仮名の踊り字（ヽ・ゝ）も書き、読む時は仮名に戻す。求められれば、どこをなぜ変えたかの理由を、文法・語・字の典拠と一緒に表で示す。OCR で読み取った当時の文書の文から、読み違いの見込みがある所（カタカナと形の似た漢字、形の似た字だけが語の表の語と違う並び、字体や仮名の混ざりなど）を、根拠付きの見直しの候補として挙げる。「文語体にして」「戦前の法令みたいに書いて」「告諭風に」「漢文訓読調に」「この文語文を現代語にして」「この古い法律の意味を教えて」「変換の理由も教えて」「解説付きで文語にして」「OCR の結果を見直して」「この翻刻の読み違いを探して」などの依頼で使う。 ([bachikawa/agent-skills](https://github.com/bachikawa/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/kundoku-bungo/skills/kundoku-bungo))
